### [101] [Eye-opening bounds on cusps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04302)
*Ryan A. Lanzetta,Ian Moult,Yifan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了共形线缺陷尖点反常维度的新非微扰不等式，基于反射正性、局域性和共形不变性对"眼睛"几何中一对尖点施加约束，得到比已知角向凹性更强的"共形凹性"条件，建立了光滑极限与融合极限之间的定量联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在获得比现有角向凹性更强的约束条件，并统一理解共形场论中线缺陷的尖点反常维度在光滑极限和融合极限之间的行为。

Method: 对由一对尖点构成的"眼睛"几何施加反射正性、局域性和共形不变性，推导出尖点反常维度满足的新不等式——共形凹性。

Result: 所得的共形凹性约束比已知的角向凹性强得多，适用于不同线缺陷间的尖点；可直接连接光滑极限与融合极限，给出缺陷改变算子维度、Casimir能量和次主导融合数据的界。

Conclusion: 揭示了共形场论中局域算子数据和线算子融合规则之间新的定量桥梁。

Abstract: We derive new nonperturbative inequalities on the cusp anomalous dimensions of conformal line defects. We impose reflection positivity, locality, and conformal invariance on a pair of cusps forming an ``eye'' geometry, deriving a novel condition on the cusp anomalous dimension which we refer to as conformal concavity. The resulting constraint is much stronger than the known angular concavity, and our derivation applies also to cusps involving distinct line defects. Remarkably, it directly links the smooth and fusion limits, yielding bounds on defect-changing operator dimensions, Casimir energies, and subleading fusion data. We thus uncover a new quantitative bridge between aspects of the local operator data and the fusion rules of line operators in conformal field theories.

</details>


### [102] [Higher-Dimensional Integrable Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04374)
*Lewis T. Cole*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文以2+1维可积手征模型中的经典孤子散射为案例，研究高维可积散射行为。该模型与Bogomolny单极子方程及自对偶Yang-Mills理论相关。延伸线孤子与团孤子及其他线孤子之间表现出非平凡的散射行为，N→N相互作用可分解为一系列2→2相互作用。三维中平行线孤子的散射与二维中团孤子散射相关，可间接与1+1维可积场论的结果进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维可积系统中的孤子散射行为，探索散射因子化性质是否在高维中成立，并与低维可积场论结果建立联系。

Method: 以2+1维可积手征模型为具体案例（该模型与Bogomolny单极子方程和自对偶Yang-Mills相关），系统研究线孤子与团孤子之间以及线孤子之间的经典散射过程，分析N→N相互作用中散射性质的分解结构。

Result: 研究发现延伸线孤子与团孤子及其他线孤子之间存在非平凡的散射行为，且N→N相互作用可以分解为一系列2→2的散射过程，表明高维情况下也保留了类似低维的因子化性质。

Conclusion: 通过三维平行线孤子散射与二维团孤子散射之间的联系，可将研究结果直接与1+1维可积场论中的已知结果进行比较，为高维可积散射理论提供了重要参考框架。

Abstract: This paper describes classical soliton scattering in the 2+1d integrable chiral model, related to the Bogomolny monopole equation and self-dual Yang-Mills, as a case study for higher-dimensional integrable scattering. Extended line solitons exhibit nontrivial scattering with lump solitons and with other line solitons, and $N \to N$ interactions can be factorised into a series of $2 \to 2$ interactions. Scattering between parallel line solitons in 3d is related to lump soliton scattering in 2d, allowing for direct comparison with results in 1+1d integrable field theories.

</details>


### [103] [Surprises of Klein paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02370)
*T. R. Ahmadov,E. T. Akhmedov,V. I. Lapushkin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了1+1维有质量狄拉克费米子在背景电场下的二次量子化理论，构建了两个方向相反平行电容器的模型（一个势能差V0>2m，另一个为V+V0且0<V<2m）。作者证明，若按上述形式瞬时开启势场，当V<2m时电流算符在t→∞的期望值为零，反驳了'每个电容器各自产生对并形成不等反向电流'的朴素观点；只有当V>2m时才能获得非零电流。此外，在含无穷墙和深阱(V0>2m)的势场中，高斯近似下极限电流期望值为零。文章讨论了这些结果与Schwinger效应及超临界核衰变的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Schwinger效应的常规物理理解，人们会认为两个反向电场电容器各自独立产生正负粒子对，形成不等且不抵消的电流。作者旨在检验这一朴素直觉是否正确，并澄清在何种条件下电流才真正非零，从而深化对强场真空不稳定性及超临界核衰变等物理现象的理解。

Method: 采用1+1维二次量子化的有质量Dirac费米子理论，将其置于分段常数的背景电场/台阶势中（由两个反向电容器构成）。通过瞬时开启势场设定初条件，解析计算电流算符在t→+∞的极限期望值。对含无穷墙与深阱的情形则使用高斯近似进行分析。

Result: 当势形式为V0>2m且0<V<2m时，瞬时开启后电流算符的极限期望值为零，表明两个电容器的Schwinger对产生效应以完全抵消的方式关联，而非独立相加。仅当V>2m时才出现非零电流。在无穷墙加深阱的配置下，高斯近似中极限电流同样为零。

Conclusion: Schwinger效应中电容器产生电流的朴素独立叠加图像是不正确的：时空上关联的真空不稳定过程可能导致宏观电流精确抵消。该结论对理解超临界核（deep well/墙势）的真空衰变与辐射机制具有重要启示。

Abstract: We consider second quantized $1+1$ dimensional Dirac fermions with mass $m$ in background electric fields. This theory mimics the following situations. We consider two parallel capacitors with opposite directions of the electric field $E$ in them. The potential energy difference in one of them is equal to $V_0 > 2m$ and in the other to $V+V_0$, with $0<V<2m$. According to general physical understanding of the Schwinger effect, each capacitor separately will create pairs and there will be oppositely directed currents, which will not be equal to each other. The total current will not vanish. In this paper we show that this is too naive an observation: the limiting expectation value of the current operator, as $t\to +\infty$, is actually zero, if the above form of the potential with $0<V<2m$ is instantaneously turned on. The non-vanishing current is obtained only if $V>2m$. We also consider the same theory in the presence of the infinite wall and a deep well $V_0>2m$. We show that in such a potential the limiting expectation value of the current is zero in the Gaussian approximation. We discuss the relation of these observations to the Schwinger effet and to the decay of supercritical nuclei.

</details>


### [104] [Boundary-Boundary Duality on Regular Black Holes, Supersymmetric Solitons and Holographic Spinning Plasma Disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04331)
*Andres Anabalon,Horatiu Nastase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了四维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论（含负宇宙学常数）中一族新的精确旋转解，描述正则旋转AdS黑洞和平滑孤子。这些几何无曲率奇异性，且在开参数区域中无闭合类时曲线；超对称极限对应因果正则的无视界构型。该解的最显著特征是具有至少两个共形（共维一）边界：一个为Minkowski时空，其能动张量的期望值描述强耦合共形等离子体的有限旋转圆盘，边缘以光速旋转；另一个为旋转黑洞几何，能动张量为零但Cotton张量非零，定义了边界处对偶引力子被固定的全息理论。研究显示对偶引力子相关的能量密度在解析延拓及将全息能标与旋转流体的洛伦兹因子等同后，精确重现了等离子体的能量密度，为边界-边界对偶提供了证据。电磁对偶在两个边界理论间交换电/磁流，构成广义镜像对称。洛伦兹因子被等同为重整化能标的电磁对偶。在适当极限下，解约化为平面Reissner—Nordström-AdS黑洞，而超对称孤子无正则静态极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造新的精确旋转AdS解，用于探索全息引力中正则旋转黑洞和光滑孤子的新物理，特别是研究多边界全息对偶（Minkowski边界与旋转黑洞Cotton边界之间）以及边界-边界对偶的可能性，以加深对全息原理和电磁对偶在引力/场论对偶中角色的理解。

Method: 在四维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论（负宇宙学常数）框架下，通过构造新的精确解析解，系统分析其几何性质（曲率奇异性、闭合类时曲线）、因果结构（超对称极限）、边界结构（双共形边界），并利用全息字典计算各边界上的能动张量与对偶引力子能量密度，通过解析延拓和全息能标与洛伦兹因子的等同关系建立边界-边界对偶，同时分析电磁对偶在两边界间的作用。

Result: 获得了一族无奇异性的精确旋转AdS解（黑洞与孤子），存在两个共形边界：Minkowski边界上的能动张量描述旋转共形等离子体圆盘；旋转黑洞边界上能动张量为零但Cotton张量非零，对应的对偶引力子能量密度经解析延拓后与等离子体能量密度精确一致。电磁对偶在两边界之间交换电/磁流，提供广义镜像对称性，洛伦兹因子被识别为重整化能标的电磁对偶。适当极限下约化为平面RN-AdS黑洞，超对称孤子无静态极限。

Conclusion: 该研究首次实现了在单个引力解中同时存在两个非平凡共形边界，并通过电磁对偶建立了边界-边界广义镜像对称性和能量密度的精确对应，这为理解引力对偶的层次结构和新颖的边界全息对应关系提供了重要突破，暗示了电磁对偶与重整化群流之间更深层的联系。

Abstract: We construct a new family of exact rotating solutions of four-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell theory with negative cosmological constant describing regular spinning AdS black holes and smooth solitons. The geometries are free of curvature singularities and admit open regions of parameter space without closed timelike curves, while supersymmetric limits correspond to causally regular horizonless configurations. A remarkable feature of these solutions is the existence of at least two codimension-one conformal boundaries. The first is Minkowski spacetime, where the expectation value of the energy--momentum tensor describes a finite spinning disk of strongly coupled conformal plasma whose edge rotates at the speed of light. The second boundary is a rotating black-hole geometry with vanishing energy--momentum tensor but non-vanishing Cotton tensor, defining a holographic theory in which the dual graviton is fixed at the boundary. We show that the energy density associated with the dual graviton exactly reproduces that of the plasma after a suitable analytic continuation and the identification of the holographic energy scale with the Lorentz factor of the rotating fluid, providing evidence for a boundary-boundary duality. Indeed, electromagnetic duality exchanges the electric and magnetic currents between the boundary supporting the graviton and that supporting the dual graviton thus providing a generalized mirror symmetry between these boundary theories. Hence, the Lorentz factor is identified with the electromagnetic dual of the renormalization scale. In an appropriate limit, the solutions reduce to the planar Reissner--Nordström--AdS black hole, whereas the supersymmetric solitons have no regular static limit.

</details>


### [105] [Superselected ghost theory: entangled pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04342)
*Bob Holdom*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将超选择规则方法推广到具有非实谱（含复共轭极点对）的鬼理论中，通过广义鬼宇称Q和交换操作R建立超选择扇区，确保物理态条件Q²|s⟩=|s⟩投影到n=0扇区，并使R超选择规则保证正概率，同时讨论光学定理对纠缠对物理切割的描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展鬼理论的超选择规则方法到包含复共轭极点对的理论，以处理非实谱情形下的量子场论一致性问题，确保概率为正并满足物理态条件。

Method: 用复质量M和M*标记两个激发态，以n（M与M*激发数之差）标记超选择扇区；定义广义鬼宇称Q赋予每个扇区相位e^{inα}；对携带实总能量动量的MM*对定义交换操作R，通过R超选择规则和R本征态（纠缠对）确保正概率。

Result: 在Q超选择下，非零范数仅出现在n=0扇区；物理态条件Q²|s⟩=|s⟩将状态投影到此扇区；R本征态为纠缠对，R超选择规则保证正概率；光学定理描述了通过这些纠缠对的物理切割。

Conclusion: 该框架为含复共轭极点对的鬼理论提供了一致的量子力学描述，通过超选择规则实现正定性概率，并为光学定理中物理切割的物理解释提供了新视角。

Abstract: The superselection-rule approach to ghost theories is extended to theories with a nonreal spectrum containing a complex-conjugate pair of poles on the physical sheet. The two excitations are labeled by their respective complex masses, $M$ and $M^*$, and the superselection sectors are labeled by $n$, the difference between the numbers of $M$ and $M^*$ excitations. The corresponding generalized ghost parity $Q$ assigns a phase $e^{inα}$ to each sector. Under $Q$ superselection, nonvanishing norms occur only in the $n=0$ sector, and the physical state condition $Q^{2}|s\rangle=|s\rangle$ projects onto this sector. For a $MM^*$ pair carrying real total energy and momentum, we define a swap operation $R$. The $R$-eigenstates are entangled pairs, and an $R$ superselection rule ensures positive probabilities. We discuss how the optical theorem describes physical cuts through these entangled pairs.

</details>


### [106] [UV/IR scale dependence in a four-derivative scalar field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04347)
*Bob Holdom*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了四导数（four-derivative）标量场理论在m→0极限下的类重整化（analog renormalization）方案，指出重整化标度μ出现在与紫外和红外发散相关的动量依赖对数中。在单圈水平上，壳上散射振幅满足一个类Callan-Symanzik方程，其中类β函数由1PI函数的UV与IR极点部分共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准重整化程序（如Callan-Symanzik方程、β函数）通常适用于二导数理论。四导数理论具有更高的导数项，其重整化性质与标准情况不同，特别是涉及同时存在的UV和IR发散结构，因此需要建立一种类比于标准重整化程序的分析框架。

Method: 作者提出了一种四导数理论的类重整化方案，将其应用于无质量极限（m→0）下的四导数标量场论。该方法通过同时考虑UV和IR发散对应的动量依赖对数中的重整化标度μ，在单圈级别构造壳上散射振幅，并推导出相应满足的类Callan-Symanzik方程，其中类β函数从1PI（单粒子不可约）函数的UV与IR极点部分联合确定。

Result: 在单圈水平上，壳上散射振幅满足类Callan-Symanzik方程，类β函数可以通过组合1PI函数的UV和IR极点部分来确定。<br><br>（注：该抽象未提供具体数值结果，主要贡献是建立了方法框架。）

Conclusion: 标准重整化程序的类比可以推广到四导数理论。在无质量极限下，重整化标度μ同时出现在UV和IR相关的动量对数中，且壳上振幅在单圈满足一个包含UV与IR贡献的类Callan-Symanzik方程，这为研究含高阶导数理论的重整化性质提供了新工具。

Abstract: An analog of the standard renormalization procedure may be useful for four-derivative theories, and we illustrate it with the four-derivative scalar field theory in the $m\to0$ limit. The renormalization scale $μ$ appears in momentum-dependent logarithms associated with both UV and IR divergences. At one loop, the on-shell scattering amplitude satisfies an analog Callan--Symanzik equation involving the analog beta functions. These functions are determined from the combined UV and IR pole parts of the 1PI functions.

</details>


### [107] [On Quasiparticles within the Refined Gribov-Zwanziger Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04423)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范理论在非微扰区域中的有效激发问题至今尚未被完全理解，需要一个更深入的理论框架来解释该区域中出现的准粒子激发行为。

Method: 采用Refined Gribov-Zwanziger（RGZ）理论作为纯规范Yang-Mills理论的有效模型，考察其二次（准粒子）激发谱，并在实质量极点的情形下利用RGZ拉氏量所具备的显式𝒫𝒯对称性来重新诠释这些准粒子激发。

Result: 提出了对RGZ理论中准粒子激发的全新物理解释，该解释在实质量极点情形下成立，并充分利用了RGZ拉氏量的𝒫𝒯对称性这一关键结构特征。

Conclusion: 通过引入𝒫𝒯对称性视角，为RGZ框架下非微扰规范理论的准粒子激发提供了一种新的理解方式，有望深化对非微扰Yang-Mills理论动力学结构的认识。

Abstract: The problem of the effective excitations of a gauge theory in the nonperturbative regime remains not completely understood. Using the Refined Gribov-Zwanziger (RGZ) theory as effective model for pure gauge Yang-Mills theories, we revisit the quasiparticle (quadratic) excitations of the theory. A novel interpretation of such quasiparticles is proposed, in the case of real mass poles, taking into account the manifest ${\cal PT}-$symmetry of the RGZ Lagrangian.

</details>


### [108] [Infrared Memory and Scrambling in a Dynamically Opened Coupled-SYK Majorana Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05336)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在将动态打开的相互作用的Majorana系统（作为超越二次极限的Majorana快门实现）与共形SYK动力学的红外结构联系起来，研究红外记忆与有限尺寸多体置乱之间的关系，探索量子临界系统中的长波长记忆保持现象。

Method: 采用大N Keldysh理论围绕共形q=4 SYK鞍点进行低频分析，研究动态双线性耦合驱动下的系统响应；同时进行精确的有限维数值模拟，对两个N=8的SYK簇研究纠缠熵、奇偶性转移和跨扇区算符置乱行为。

Result: 发现快门打开过程具有强烈的红外记忆保留，异常响应随红外截断的降低而更加敏感；数值模拟确认在打开后系统表现出增强的扇区间纠缠、奇偶性转移和跨扇区算符置乱。

Conclusion: 结果表明动态打开的相互作用Majorana系统能够在二次极限之外保留长波长记忆，将共形SYK动力学的红外结构与有限尺寸多体系统的置乱行为建立了联系。

Abstract: We study a dynamically opened Majorana junction formed by two coupled Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) systems. A time-dependent bilinear coupling drives the system from a nearly decoupled to a strongly hybridized regime, providing an interacting realization of a Majorana shutter. Using a low-frequency analysis of the large-$N$ Keldysh theory around the conformal $q=4$ SYK saddle, we find that the opening retains a strong infrared memory, with the associated anomalous response becoming increasingly sensitive to the infrared cutoff. Exact finite-dimensional simulations of two $N=8$ SYK clusters show enhanced inter-sector entanglement, parity transfer, and cross-sector operator scrambling after the opening. These results connect the infrared structure of conformal SYK dynamics with finite-size many-body scrambling and show how a dynamically opened interacting Majorana system can retain long-wavelength memory beyond the quadratic limit.

</details>

Conclusion: 余伴随轨道方法可以推广到无限维手征代数领域，为二维（超）共形场论的特征标计算提供统一的几何框架，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We explain a general procedure to compute characters of chiral algebras of 2d (S)CFTs geometrically from their coadjoint orbits, in analogy with the coadjoint orbit method for finite-dimensional Lie groups. ### [110] [Open-Closed-Open Triality for 1/2 BPS Three-Point Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04621)
*Keisuke Konosu,Kenta Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中发展了针对受保护半BPS三点函数的开-闭-开三对偶性。通过从表示巨引力子的行列式插入出发，构建了V型开弦高斯三矩阵模型，并通过颜色-味道变换导出了由三个双基本矩阵形成的三角quiver的F型开弦模型。作者推导了精确的有限N单巨多项式，恢复了大N鞍点，并通过组合数据的显式双射证明了V型和F型特征展开的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开-闭-开三对偶（open-closed-open triality）此前主要应用于关联两个不同开弦描述的系统，本文旨在将该框架扩展到四维N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中受保护半BPS三点函数，以深入理解巨引力子、开弦和闭弦描述之间的对应关系，特别是研究在两点情形中不存在的定向三次圈的闭弦quiver行走等新特征。

Method: 作者首先从行列式插入（代表巨引力子）出发构建V型开弦高斯三矩阵模型；随后通过颜色-味道变换（color-flavor transformation）导出F型开弦模型，该模型由三个双基本矩阵构成的三角quiver描述。作者推导了精确的有限N单巨多项式，恢复了已知的F型大N鞍点，并通过组合数据（V型和F型特征展开之间）的显式双射证明了其等价性。在闭弦角点，连通的三矩阵收缩被映射为三色整数Strebel曲面，并提出对应的靶空间构造可由重心细分Belyi映射或彩色Hurwitz星座来描述。

Result: 成功建立了V型和F型开弦描述之间的等价性，通过显式双射证明了二者的特征展开等价；恢复了已知的F型大N鞍点并推导了精确的有限N单巨多项式。闭弦侧的三色整数Strebel曲面与Belyi映射/Hurwitz星座建立了联系，揭示了三点情形中特有的定向三次圈结构。

Conclusion: 本文完成了开-闭-开三对偶在N=4超杨-米尔斯半BPS三点函数中的建立与验证，展示了V型与F型开弦模型通过闭弦理论（Strebel曲面/Belyi映射/Hurwitz星座）关联的三重描述结构，为该领域的进一步研究（如更高点函数或更一般巨引力子的推广）奠定了基础。

Abstract: Open-closed-open triality relates two distinct open string descriptions through a common closed string theory. In this paper, we develop an open-closed-open triality for protected half-BPS three-point functions in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory. Starting from determinant insertions representing giant gravitons, we formulate a V-type open-string Gaussian three-matrix model and, through a color-flavor transformation, derive an F-type open-string model of three bifundamental matrices forming a triangular quiver. Its closed quiver walks include oriented cubic cycles, a feature absent from the bipartite two-point case. We test the correspondence by deriving exact finite-$N$ single-giant polynomials, recovering the known F-type large-$N$ saddles, and proving the equivalence of the V- and F-type character expansions via an explicit bijection of their combinatorial data. At the closed string corner, connected three-matrix contractions determine three-colored integer Strebel surfaces. We argue that the corresponding target-space construction is naturally described by a barycentrically subdivided Belyi map or a colored Hurwitz constellation.

</details>


### [111] [Hironaka Geometry and Resurgence in Finite-$N$ Matrix Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04664)
*Robert de Mello Koch,Vinayak Raj,Anik Rudra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限N不变理论如何组织矩阵模型的非微扰结构。对于四个无迹厄米2×2矩阵的模型，Hironaka分解将规范不变构型空间实现为初级不变量空间的八叶分支覆盖。研究表明该覆盖的分支点自然产生额外的鞍点，这些鞍点控制微扰理论的主Borel奇异性和大阶增长，其首阶涨落修正无需拟合参数即可重现首个次领头大阶修正，为有限N不变几何与复苏（resurgence）之间建立了具体联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解矩阵模型中非微扰效应的完整结构是量子场论和数学物理的核心问题之一。虽然复苏理论为微扰与非微扰部分提供了系统性框架，但其几何起源尚不清晰。作者旨在通过有限N不变论——特别是Hironaka分解——揭示非微扰鞍点的几何来源，从而在不变几何与复苏之间架起桥梁。

Method: 采用Hironaka分解研究四个无迹厄米2×2矩阵的规范不变构型空间，将其描述为初级不变量空间上的八叶分支覆盖；分析分支点（ramification points）如何生成附加鞍点；对显式秩二鞍点计算作用量、单圈归一化以及与微扰真空的Picard-Lefschetz连接。

Result: 该鞍点精确控制主Borel奇异性和微扰理论的大阶增长；鞍点的首阶涨落修正无需任何拟合参数即可复现首个次领头大阶修正，验证了理论预言与直接微扰计算的一致性。

Conclusion: 有限N不变几何中的分支结构天然编码了复苏理论中关键的鞍点信息，为矩阵模型中非微扰结构的几何理解提供了新范式，确立了不变几何与复苏之间的具体对应关系。

Abstract: We study how finite-$N$ invariant theory organizes the non-perturbative structure of matrix models. For a model of four traceless Hermitian $2\times 2$ matrices, the Hironaka decomposition realizes the gauge-invariant configuration space as an eight-sheeted branched cover of the space of primary invariants. We show that ramification points of this cover naturally generate additional saddle points when the action depends only on the primaries. For an explicit rank-two saddle, we compute its action, one-loop normalization and Picard--Lefschetz connection to the perturbative vacuum. The same saddle controls the leading Borel singularity and large-order growth of perturbation theory, while its first fluctuation correction reproduces the first subleading large-order correction with no fitted parameters. Our results provide a concrete link between finite-$N$ invariant geometry and resurgence in matrix models.

</details>


### [112] [Form Factor Alphabets and Antipodal Duality from Cluster Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04839)
*Song He,Jiahao Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文为平面N=4超Yang-Mills理论中的手征应力张量form factor提出了一种基于cluster代数的符号字母表描述框架。通过折叠带两周期冻结束的Gr(4,2n) quiver，建立了n点form factor字母表的周期框架。四点情形下折叠得到具有无限多cluster变量的widetilde{VI}簇代数，通过热带截断和反极点闭包筛选出有限的代数字母空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为手征应力张量form factor的符号字母表提供系统的cluster-代数描述，特别是基于三维C_2 quiver展开，建立n点推广的周期框架。

Method: 提出基于折叠Gr(4,2n) quiver的周期框架（含两周期冻结束）；利用热带截断选择有限理性坐标和物理极限射线；通过A_1^{(1)}突变序列生成四类平方根与对应代数字母空间；在宇称保持面上，通过进一步折叠，用两次交换突变加重新标记实现反极点映射；寻找同一突变类中的自反极点quiver。

Result: 四点情形的折叠给出widetilde{VI}簇代数（无限多簇变量）。热带截断选出有限集理性坐标和四条物理极限射线，生成四个平方根。反极点闭包精确排除八个额外字母。找到同突变类中的自反极点quiver，其双/三重共线边界分别复现三点form factor的C_2代数与六点振幅的A_3代数；折叠后者至C_2后反极点映射交换两个C_2边界。

Conclusion: 建立了手征应力张量form factor的cluster-代数字母表新框架，并揭示了反极点自对偶性与多重共线极限下与振幅/低点form factor簇代数结构之间深刻的关联。

Abstract: We develop a cluster-algebraic description of symbol alphabets for chiral stress-tensor form factors in planar $\mathcal N=4$ super-Yang--Mills theory. Motivated by unfolding the three-point $C_2$ quiver, we propose a periodic framework for $n$-point form-factor alphabets based on folding a $\mathrm{Gr}(4,2n)$ quiver with two periods of frozen nodes. At four points, the folding leads to the $\widetilde{\mathrm{VI}}$ cluster algebra, which has infinitely many cluster variables. Tropical truncation selects a finite set of rational coordinates and four physical limit rays. The $A_1^{(1)}$ mutation sequences approaching these rays generate the four square roots and the corresponding algebraic-letter spaces. Within the resulting finite set of rational candidates, antipodal closure excludes precisely eight additional letters. On the parity-preserving surface, the antipodal map is realized, after a further folding, by two commuting mutations followed by a relabelling. Furthermore, we find an alternative quiver within the same mutation class that becomes self-antipodal after parity folding, mirroring the antipodal self-duality of the four-point MHV form factor. Its double- and triple-collinear boundaries reproduce, respectively, the $C_2$ algebra of the three-point form factor and the $A_3$ algebra of the six-point amplitude; after folding the latter to $C_2$, the antipodal map exchanges the two $C_2$ boundaries.

</details>


### [113] [What is Classical Null String Theory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04844)
*M. M. Sheikh-Jabbari,H. Yavartanoo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视D维Minkowski靶空间中零弦（null string）的定义，区分了一致的部分规范化的Carrollian sigma模型与零弦理论，指出在零张力极限下对Carroll-Weyl对称性进行规范化的必要性，从而为将ILST理论转化为真正的零弦理论提供了补充条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典零弦的定义需要重新审视。ILST作用量虽然规范了世界膜微分同胚和共同的Weyl标度，但未规范化内在Carrollian结构中时间与空间表示之间的独立相对标度（即Carroll-Weyl标度）。一个物理的弦理论要求所有局部的世界膜表示变化都被规范化，因此需要澄清零弦理论与部分规范化的Carrollian sigma模型之间的区别。

Method: 本文通过分析ILST作用量中未规范化的Carroll-Weyl对称性，考察有限张力下该规范化所遇到的阻碍，以及在零张力极限下规范化如何生成额外的第一类约束，并沿规范轨道识别物理构型。同时将零世界膜描述为零测地线的同余，从靶空间视角提供对Carroll-Weyl规范化性质的互补解释。

Result: 研究发现在最小实现中，Carroll-Weyl对称性的规范化在有限张力下受到阻碍，而在零张力下则产生一个额外的第一类约束和相关物理构型的规范轨道识别。零世界膜可作为零测地线同余的描述，为Carroll-Weyl标度的规范化提供了靶空间层面的一致解释。

Conclusion: ILST理论并非完整的零弦理论，而是部分规范化的Carrollian sigma模型。要将其真正转变为零弦理论，需要对Carroll-Weyl对称性进行规范化，在零张力下实现的这一规范化提供了补充要求，从而完善了零弦理论的经典描述。

Abstract: We revisit the classical definition of a null string in a D-dimensional Minkowski target space and distinguish a consistent partially-gauged Carrollian sigma-model from the null string theory. The Isberg-Lindström-Sundborg-Theodoridis (ILST) action gauges worldsheet diffeomorphisms and the common Weyl rescaling, but not the independent relative rescaling of the temporal and spatial representatives of the intrinsic Carrollian structure, Carroll-Weyl scaling. A physical string theory requires every local change of worldsheet representative to be gauged, therefore, the Carroll-Weyl symmetry should be gauged in a null string theory. In the minimal realization, the corresponding gauging is obstructed at finite tension, whereas at zero tension it yields an additional first-class constraint and the associated identification of physical configurations along its gauge orbits. The description of the null worldsheet as a congruence of null geodesics provides a complementary target-space interpretation of gauging of the Carroll-Weyl scaling. Our analysis thus provides the supplementary requirements that turns the ILST theory into the null string theory.

</details>


### [114] [de Sitter and S-folds in type IIB from $\mathcal{N} = 4$ $D = 4$ gauged supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05004)
*Colin Sterckx*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文对N=4 D=4纯SO(4)规范超重力中允许dS4解的type IIB提升进行分类，并通过对内空间进行S-fold商化，构造了type IIB超引力的经典de Sitter解，且具有有限非零的四维有效牛顿常数，绕过了Maldacena-Nunez no-go定理的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找type IIB超引力中经典的de Sitter解是弦论宇宙学的重要问题，但Maldacena-Nunez no-go定理排除了许多简单构造的可能性。该工作旨在通过S-fold技术规避no-go定理假设，构造具有有限四维有效牛顿常数的经典dS解。

Method: 对N=4 D=4纯SO(4)规范超引力的type IIB提升进行分类。内几何由两个S^2纤维化于黎曼面Σ上构成，由Σ上的一对调和函数h_{1,2}刻画。选取Σ=R×I上的特定调和函数，沿内空间的非紧致方向进行S-fold商化，以规避Maldacena-Nunez no-go定理的假设条件。

Result: 获得了type IIB超引力中一组新的de Sitter解，这些解的内几何由双S^2纤维化结构描述，且通过S-fold构造确保四维有效牛顿常数有限且非零，从而达成经典的、自发紧化的dS解。

Conclusion: 该工作通过S-fold商化构造了type IIB超引力的经典de Sitter解，突破了Maldacena-Nunez no-go定理的适用条件，为弦论中dS解的构造提供了新的途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we classify the type IIB uplifts of the $\mathcal{N}=4$ $D=4$ pure $\text{SO}(4)$-gauged supergravity admitting a dS$_4$ solution. As for their AdS cousins, the internal geometries of these uplifts consist of two $S^2$ fibred over a Riemann surface $Σ$, and are classified by pairs of harmonic functions $h_{1,\,2}$ on $Σ$, providing a new family of de Sitter solutions in type IIB. Choosing specific functions $h_{1,\,2}$ on $Σ= \mathbb{R} \times I$, we perform an S-fold quotient of the internal space along its non-compact direction. This S-folding allows us to evade the assumptions of the Maldacena-Nunez no-go theorem and provides a classical de Sitter solution of type IIB supergravity with finite and non-vanishing four-dimensional effective Newton constant.

</details>


### [115] [The endless journey towards the horizon of a quantum black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05164)
*Victor Franken,Thomas G. Mertens,Bruno de S. L. Torres*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究量子引力中黑洞视界附近的量子效应，指出强混沌（随机矩阵谱统计）特性使得引力穿戴的坠落观察者需要无限的原时才能到达视界，因此黑洞内部对外部观察者而言在操作上不可达，打破了引力中经典的大N因果结构。通过2d量子黑洞背景中坠落粒子探测器的响应研究，发现探测器在接近视界进入无穷长非微扰旅程时激发概率出现峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基础量子引力问题：量子效应是否会在黑洞视界附近产生非平凡结构，以及黑洞内部是否只是大N极限下的涌现特征。这关乎我们对黑洞内部物理和视界附近因果结构的根本理解。

Method: 利用随机矩阵理论刻画黑洞谱的强混沌统计性质，分析引力穿戴的坠落观察者的原时演化；并在2维量子黑洞背景下，通过粒子探测器与物质场耦合的模型来模拟坠落观察者的经历，计算其激发概率响应。

Result: 发现黑洞的强混沌特性使得坠落观察者需要无限原时才能到达视界，黑洞内部在操作上不可达；坠落探测器在接近视界时出现激发概率的峰值，对应其进入靠近视界的无穷长非微扰旅程。

Conclusion: 黑洞内部的经典（大N）因果结构在量子引力中发生尖锐的失效，外部观察者无法在操作上进入黑洞内部，黑洞内部可能仅是大N极限下的涌现现象。

Abstract: It is a problem of great importance in quantum gravity whether quantum effects lead to nontrivial structure near black hole horizons, and if their interiors are emergent features of a large $N$ limit. We show that the strongly chaotic nature of black holes, captured by random matrix statistics of their spectrum, makes it so that a suitably gravitationally dressed infalling observer takes an infinite amount of proper time to reach the horizon. This makes the black hole interior operationally inaccessible to any outside observer, marking a sharp breakdown of the large $N$ causal structure of black holes in quantum gravity. To model the experience of an infalling observer, we study the response of an infalling particle detector coupled to a matter field in a 2d quantum black hole background, finding a peak in the detector's excitation probability as it enters an infinitely long non-perturbative journey close to the black hole horizon.

</details>


### [116] [Decompactification and the Flat-Space Limit of AdS$_5\times$ S$^5$ Master Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05409)
*Burkhard Eden,Paul Heslop,Harshal Kulkarni,Arthur Lipstein,Romain Ruzziconi,Akshay Yelleshpur Srikant*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究AdS5/CFT4全息关联函数的平直空间极限，同时考虑内部S^5的紧致化解除。作者表明该极限可自然地用"主传播子"和"主关联函数"表述，它们能重求和无穷多的Kaluza-Klein模式。边界关联函数的结果可从5维受限运动学的散射振幅角度解释；而将外部插入点保留在AdS5×S^5体内部再取极限，则可恢复10维闵可夫斯基空间中无运动学限制的平直空间振幅。最后作者证明，若对球面进行解析延拓使几何变为AdS5×dS5，则平直空间极限与边界极限可交换，得到的公式可用双时间方向平直空间散射振幅（满足两个同时为零约束的10维运动学）来解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望深入理解AdS/CFT的平直空间极限，特别是同时解紧致化内部球面所带来的效应。此前关于平直空间极限的研究通常只考虑边界关联函数或保留内部紧致化的简化情形，而该工作旨在系统处理KK模式无穷塔的求和问题，并探讨平直空间全息性（flat-space holography）及其与CFT的Carrollian极限之间的联系。

Method: 论文提出用"主传播子"和"主关联函数"框架来表述AdS5×S^5的关联函数，这些对象自动重求和无穷KK塔。作者区分两种情形：(1) 在边界取关联函数后取平直空间极限，得到5维受限运动学的振幅；(2) 在AdS5×S^5体中取关联函数再取极限，恢复10维无约束的平直空间振幅。同时还考虑将S^5解析延拓为dS^5，使得平直空间极限与边界极限可交换，此时所得振幅具有双时间方向并满足两个零约束。

Result: 建立了统一的主关联函数形式体系，阐明了几种不同极限的交换关系：当S^5保持紧致（未被延拓）时，边界极限与平直空间极限的顺序影响所得振幅的维数（5d vs 10d）；而通过解析延拓为dS^5可恢复两种极限的交换性，得到10维双时间运动学下的振幅公式。

Conclusion: 这些构造对平直空间全息性有重要启示，尤其是在与Carrollian极限的关系上。作者暗示，主关联函数框架可能为理解CFT的Carrollian极限如何编码10维平直空间散射振幅提供新的路径，同时展示了内部流形紧致化在极限过程中的关键作用。

Abstract: We study the flat-space limit of AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$ holographic correlators while accounting for the simultaneous decompactification of the internal S$^5$. We show that this limit is naturally formulated in terms of master propagators and correlators, which resum the infinite tower of Kaluza--Klein modes. For boundary correlators, the resulting expressions can be interpreted in terms of amplitudes with kinematics restricted to 5d. We then consider master correlators whose external insertions are kept in the AdS$_5\times$S$^5$ bulk before taking the limit, and argue that they recover flat-space amplitudes with unrestricted kinematics in 10d Minkowski space. Finally, we show that the flat-space limit commutes with the boundary limit if we analytically continue the sphere so that the bulk geometry becomes AdS$_5 \times$dS$_5$, and the resulting formulae suggest an interpretation in terms of amplitudes in flat space with two time directions and with 10d kinematics satisfying two simultaneous null constraints. We discuss the implications of these constructions for flat-space holography and its relation to the Carrollian limit of the dual CFT.

</details>

### [117] [Derived Associative Algebras and Cyclic Cohomology of Q-manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04805)
*Andrew James Bruce*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Q流形（配备奇向量场的超流形）框架下重新审视Loday导子积，通过平移分次将其解释为标准乘积，从而为任意Q流形构造了导子（非交换）结合的Z2-分次代数。作者将Connes循环上同调应用于该导子结合代数，定义了Q流形上区别于标准上同调的新上同调——导子循环上同调，并将其中的导子循环余循环解释为BV-BFV-BRST形式论中的经典BRST不变泛函，或应用于正则叶理（经由其叶理李代数胚）时的广义Ruelle-Sullivan流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Q流形是现代数学物理中的重要结构，其标准上同调（Q上同调）已被广泛研究。然而，Loday导子积在Q流形背景下的几何与代数意义尚未被充分挖掘。作者旨在通过重新解释导子积的结构，建立Q流形与Hochschild/循环上同调理论（Connes理论）之间的新联系，从而揭示Q流形更丰富的代数结构，并为BV-BF V-BRST形式论及叶理理论提供新的不变量。

Method: 作者采用以下技术路线：(1) 将Loday导子积（Grassmann奇乘积）通过平移分次技巧重新解释为超流形上的标准乘积，从而证明该乘积满足结合的Z2-分次（非交换）代数公理；(2) 基于此导子结合代数，自然地应用Connes的循环上同调理论构造新上同调；(3) 将所得导子循环余循环在BV-BFV-BRST框架下解释为经典BRST不变泛函；(4) 针对正则叶理，经由叶理李代数胚将该构造应用于广义Ruelle-Sullivan流。

Result: 论文建立了Q流形上的导子结合代数结构，导出了区别于标准Q上同调的导子循环上同调理论。这些新余循环在物理上与BRST不变泛函自然地对应，在几何上则与Ruelle-Sullivan流建立了联系，从而实现了代数、几何与物理三个层面构造的统一。

Conclusion: 该工作表明Loday导子积在Q流形上并非孤立的代数结构，而是可以经过分次平移进入标准结合代数框架，进而应用循环上同调工具。这为Q流形提供了一套全新的不变量系统，并建立了与BV-BFV-BRST量子化方案及叶理几何的深刻联系，暗示了导子循环上同调在此类理论中可能扮演具有物理意义的重要角色。

Abstract: Loday's derived product is revisited in the setting of Q-manifolds, i.e., supermanifolds equipped with an odd vector field that `squares to zero'. We interpret the Grassmann odd product as a standard product upon shifting the grading, which implies the existence of a derived (noncommutative) associative $\mathbb{Z}_2$-graded algebra associated with any Q-manifold. We apply Connes' cyclic cohomology to the derived associative algebra, giving a new cohomology on Q-manifolds distinct from the standard cohomology: we refer to this as the derived cyclic cohomology of a Q-manifold. The derived cyclic cocycles are interpreted as classically BRST-invariant functionals within the BV--BFV--BRST formalism or generalised Ruelle--Sullivan currents when applied to regular foliations via their foliation Lie algebroids.

</details>


### [118] [Finite-Corner Reconstruction and Separation for Cones of Normal Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04889)
*Mohsen Kian,Fuad Kittaneh*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了介于有界线性算子代数B(H)与B(K)之间正规映射凸锥的有限角框架，证明点-超弱闭的切-填（cut-pad）稳定锥完全由其有限维角锥决定；任意一致族的闭有限维角锥都有唯一的全局实现。对非点-超弱闭的切-填稳定锥，从角锥的范数闭包重建恰好得到其点-超弱闭包。结合有限维分离与Choi表示，全局锥的非成员性总可在单个有限维角上被有限维验证子检测到，并用完全正映射与可分解映射进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究算子代数之间正规映射构成的凸锥（如完全正映射、可分解映射等）时，希望建立一种有限维约化策略，使无穷维全局性质可以通过有限维角锥来刻画和判定，从而将有限维工具（如分离定理、Choi表示）运用于无穷维凸锥的成员资格判定问题。

Method: 引入切-填（cut-pad）稳定性的有限维角锥框架。对点-超弱闭锥证明可由有限维角锥完全重构，反之对一致族的闭有限维角锥给出唯一的全局实现；对一般的切-填稳定锥通过范数闭包重建得到其点-超弱闭包。结合Choi表示与有限维分离定理构造单有限维角上的验证子。

Result: 建立了切-填稳定锥与其有限维角锥之间的双射对应（对点-超弱闭情形），并证明了一般情形下可由有限维角锥的范数闭包恢复点-超弱闭包。进一步证明了全局锥外点总存在谱单个有限维角的有限维验证子；可分解映射情形下验证子的Hermitian部分可选取为PPT。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究无穷维算子空间上的凸锥提供了系统的有限维约化方法论，确立了有限维验证子的完备性，并示范了其在完全正映射与可分解映射中的应用价值。

Abstract: We develop a finite-corner framework for convex cones of normal maps between \(\mathcal B(\mathcal H)\) and \(\mathcal B(\mathcal K)\). The basic structural assumption is stability under finite cut--pad operations. We prove that every point-ultraweakly closed cut--pad stable cone is completely determined by its finite-dimensional corner cones, and conversely that every coherent family of closed finite-dimensional corner cones admits a unique global realization of this type. For a cut--pad stable cone that is not necessarily point-ultraweakly closed, reconstruction from the norm closures of its corner cones yields exactly its point-ultraweak closure. Combining this reconstruction principle with finite-dimensional separation and the Choi representation, we show that non-membership in a global cone is always detected on a single finite-dimensional corner by a finite-dimensional witness. We illustrate the framework for completely positive and decomposable maps; in the decomposable case, the Hermitian parts of the finite-corner witnesses are PPT.

</details>


### [119] [Representation Theory of Canonical Commutation Relations Arising from the Quantization of the Klein-Gordon Equation with an External Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05237)
*Yasumichi Matsuzawa,Itaru Sasaki,Akito Suzuki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子化大质量标量场在外势影响下，规范对易关系（CCR）的Weyl表示，探讨当质量或势变化时Weyl表示是否保持等价。研究表明质量不同时Weyl表示必不等价；质量相同时，等价的转变阈值出现在势差衰减指数为-3/2处，与散射理论中短程条件对应的-1衰减率形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论中，不同的物理参数（如质量、外势）对应不同的Fock表示。确定Weyl表示之间是否（不）等价，是理解量子场论中真空态和粒子解释的根本问题，也是处理非微扰场论和曲率背景中场量子化的重要基础。

Method: 将Weyl表示等价性问题转化为对Schrödinger算子的谱分析问题，利用Schrödinger算子的谱性质来判定两个Weyl表示之间的幺正等价性。

Result: 主要结论有二：（1）当质量不同时，对应的Weyl表示是不等价的；（2）当质量相同时，势差函数的衰减指数决定等价性——等价与不等价之间的临界衰减率为-3/2，这不同于散射理论中定义短程条件的衰减率-1。

Conclusion: Weyl表示的（不）等价性与质量差异和外势差异的渐近行为密切相关。临界衰减指数-3/2与散射理论中的-1之间的差异表明，CCR表示的等价性条件比散射理论中的短程条件更严格，这揭示了QFT中表示不等价性发生的物理尺度对比。

Abstract: We investigate the Weyl representation of the canonical commutation relations for a model describing a quantized massive scalar field under the influence of an external potential. The main problem is to determine whether the Weyl representation remains equivalent to or becomes inequivalent to the original one when the mass and/or potential are changed. This problem is reduced to the study of Schrödinger operators. It turns out that the Weyl representations are inequivalent when the masses differ. Moreover, when the masses are the same, the transition between equivalence and inequivalence occurs at the decay rate $-3/2$ of the difference between the potentials. This contrasts with the decay rate $-1$ that defines the short-range condition in scattering theory.

</details>
